# 01 — ARCHITECTURE LOCKS

## Jarvis

Jarvis is the owner-facing Chief of Staff and mediation layer.

Jarvis:
- understands owner intent;
- gathers constrained state;
- briefs and prioritizes;
- allocates work;
- requests explicit approvals;
- coordinates Agent 0 and bounded specialists;
- explains evidence, uncertainty, conflicts and health.

Jarvis does **not** own:
- raw business truth;
- credentials;
- production authority;
- tenant data;
- workflow durability;
- continuous cognition.

**The infrastructure stays aware. Jarvis wakes when judgment is useful.**

## Agent 0

Agent 0 is the tenant-bound logical business operator.

Each business tenant has one logical Agent 0 identity.

It is instantiated from durable tenant state when work arrives and cannot grant itself authority.

## Hard tenant isolation — normative

`tenant_id` is a non-bypassable security/data/execution/authority boundary.

### Primary enforcement

V1 MUST use **PostgreSQL Row-Level Security** on every tenant-owned table, with:
- RLS enabled;
- `FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY` where applicable;
- runtime DB roles that are not superusers, not table owners, and do not have `BYPASSRLS`;
- transaction-local tenant context set by trusted server infrastructure;
- tenant-aware relational constraints/foreign keys where applicable.

Application-layer filtering is **defense in depth only** and MUST NOT be the sole tenant boundary.

### Runtime rule

The model/client cannot choose or override `tenant_id`.

Tenant scope is created by trusted infrastructure and carried through:
- tasks,
- workflows,
- workers,
- retrieval,
- connectors,
- credentials,
- events,
- receipts,
- approvals,
- Agent 0 routines.

## Confidentiality model

Tenants are classified at minimum as:

- `FIRST_PARTY_PORTFOLIO`
- `THIRD_PARTY_ISOLATED`

The owner may intentionally synthesize across authorized `FIRST_PARTY_PORTFOLIO` tenants.

A `THIRD_PARTY_ISOLATED` tenant:
- is processed tenant-locally;
- does not influence another tenant's customer-facing output or decision context;
- does not contribute raw data to global durable memory;
- may contribute only explicitly permitted, de-identified aggregate operational metadata.

## Cross-business owner view

`owner request`
→ resolve authorized first-party portfolio
→ independent tenant-bound fan-out
→ typed tenant receipts
→ global synthesis.

Raw tenant context is not persisted globally.

## Authority

Permanent rule:

> **Owner grants authority. Jarvis allocates already-granted authority to work. Workers use it. Policy limits it. Trusted executors enforce it.**

“Allocates” means attenuation/routing of an existing valid grant. Jarvis cannot mint, widen or reactivate authority.

Natural language cannot create or expand authority.

## Owner authentication

Owner identity is part of the root of trust.

V1 requires:
- authenticated owner session;
- MFA enrollment;
- short-lived step-up MFA for high-risk grants/approvals;
- exact proposal-bound approval;
- session revocation/logout;
- secure account recovery.

High-risk approval in V1 = **owner + step-up MFA**.

No mandatory two-human approval in V1.

## Memory / state / truth

- External provider = authority for its own external object.
- Postgres = canonical AgencyOS normalized dynamic state + decisions + provenance + projections.
- Git/GitHub = canonical versioned procedures/skills/policies/prompts/tests.
- Object storage = raw evidence.
- DBOS = V1 durable workflow execution state.
- pgvector/FTS = rebuildable retrieval indexes.
- Hermes = replaceable conversation/runtime + tiny hot memory.
- Obsidian = optional human knowledge surface.

Conversation history, summaries and vector similarity are evidence/context — not authority.

## Events / proactivity

`authenticated webhook / stream / poll / reconciliation`
→ normalize
→ dedupe
→ materialize state
→ freshness/conflict
→ materiality
→ `SILENCE | BATCH | NOTIFY | WAKE`.

Unauthenticated or failed-auth inbound events cannot materialize canonical state.

## Non-silenceable classes

These classes cannot be reduced to SILENCE by an LLM/materiality heuristic:

- tenant-isolation/security event;
- credential/authentication anomaly;
- authority/permission change;
- kill-switch/fail-closed event;
- material spend/refund/discount/price/commitment event;
- opt-out/legal/privacy request;
- customer-facing effect with unknown/ambiguous result;
- provider/control-store outage affecting safe execution;
- verified severe production fault.

They must at least enter owner-visible attention state, with notification severity determined by deterministic policy.

## Specialists

Default = one agent.

V1 specialist contracts:
- Scout
- Analyst
- Builder
- Operator
- Reviewer

Specialists are available capabilities, not mandatory pipeline stages.

No:
- permanent swarm;
- free recursive delegation;
- unrestricted peer chat;
- runtime-created privileged specialist types;
- uncontrolled parallel writers.

## Tools / hands

Preferred execution surface:

1. direct API/SDK;
2. approved API-backed MCP/Hermes tool;
3. structured CLI/code;
4. deterministic DOM/browser automation;
5. model-driven browser automation;
6. full computer use / Orgo;
7. human.

A weaker fallback cannot be used after an ambiguous write unless the first effect is proven absent.

## Trusted executor

All material external effects pass through the trusted executor.

No verified receipt = no claim that a material action succeeded.

## V1 durability

DBOS + Postgres.

Internal workflow durability does not create exactly-once external side effects.

External effects require deterministic idempotency and/or observable postcondition verification.

## Fail-closed rule

If authority/kill-state cannot be freshly verified, material writes are denied.

Control-plane degradation never causes fail-open execution.

## Coding factory

Use Cursor as primary builder and Codex as independent reviewer/fallback.

Use native execution runtimes and GitHub gates rather than building a giant custom coding-agent runtime.

## Mac Mini

The Mac Mini remains the trusted owner build/control host.


---FILE---
# 05 — PRODUCT BEHAVIOR

## Jarvis

Jarvis is the owner-facing Chief of Staff.

Default briefing:

**Changed → Needs You → Risks / Unknowns → Completed → Evidence**

No material delta = silence, except non-silenceable classes defined by policy.

## Briefing integrity / hostile content

Owner-facing briefing claims must be generated from typed, evidence-backed records.

Content originating from untrusted sources such as:
- customer messages,
- email,
- webpages,
- documents,
- tool output,
- retrieved memory,
- model output

must retain provenance/trust metadata.

When untrusted text itself matters, Jarvis presents it as attributed/quoted content, not as a trusted instruction or unexplained system fact.

A briefing cannot create authority.

## Owner controls

`ADD_CONTEXT`
`STEER_NEXT`
`PAUSE`
`APPROVE`
`REJECT`
`STOP`
`RESUME`

Inspection is passive.

`APPROVE` / `REJECT` applies only to the exact proposal shown to the owner and is bound to:
- `proposal_id`,
- canonical `request_hash`,
- current relevant state/version,
- authenticated owner session,
- step-up MFA when policy requires.

## Owner authentication

High-risk approvals require a recent step-up MFA.

V1 has one human root of trust: the owner.
No mandatory second approver.

## Agent 0

Agent 0 is the tenant-bound logical business operator.

Core loop:

`EVENT`
→ refresh/reconcile current state
→ single-flight subject/routine decision
→ gather minimum context
→ propose
→ deterministic policy/risk
→ execute if authorized
→ verify
→ receipt
→ update state
→ evaluate/escalate

## Agent 0 autonomy

| Level | Behavior |
|---|---|
| T0 | observe |
| T1 | recommend/draft |
| T2 | structured low-risk reversible/pre-authorized work |
| T3 | customer-facing action inside approved routine |
| T4 | commercial/high-risk action only under explicit grant/cap + owner step-up MFA where required |
| T5 | prohibited/owner-only |

Price changes, discounts, refunds, contracts, credentials, security/permission changes and uncapped spend never become routine authority accidentally.

## Agent 0 single-flight

For customer-facing decisioning, only one active decision workflow may exist for the same:

`tenant_id + subject_ref + routine_id + logical_stage`

Additional events update/queue behind that workflow rather than spawning competing decisions.

## Cross-business confidentiality

Jarvis may synthesize across the owner's first-party businesses.

Outside customer/client tenants remain isolated:
- no raw cross-client context;
- no client A content influencing client B customer-facing output;
- no cross-client global durable memory.

## Deletion behavior

If a customer data deletion request is valid:
- identifiable data is removed from canonical and derived stores;
- vectors/FTS/cache/derived summaries are withdrawn/rebuilt;
- raw PII is not preserved inside immutable receipt payloads;
- only non-identifying audit proof remains unless legally required retention applies.

# 06 — SYSTEM CONTRACTS

**THIS FILE IS THE SINGLE CANONICAL CONTRACT/SCHEMA AUTHORITY.**

All code, migrations, tests and other SOT files reference the versioned contracts defined here.

## Contract versioning

Every machine contract has:
- `contract_name`
- `contract_version`
- schema file in Git
- immutable Git SHA for a release
- backward/forward compatibility declaration where relevant.

Running workflows pin contract versions.

## ContextEnvelope

```yaml
contract_version: 1
request_id: uuid
principal_id: string
authorized_tenant_ids: [uuid]
active_tenant_id: uuid|null
tenant_confidentiality_class: FIRST_PARTY_PORTFOLIO|THIRD_PARTY_ISOLATED|null
context_source: explicit_selector|explicit_language|unique_entity|recent_explicit_context|portfolio
cross_tenant: boolean
context_epoch: integer
confidence: exact|high|ambiguous
```

Material writes require exact tenant resolution.

## OwnerAuthContext

```yaml
contract_version: 1
owner_principal_id: string
session_id: string
auth_strength: standard|step_up_mfa
authenticated_at: timestamp
step_up_verified_at: timestamp|null
step_up_expires_at: timestamp|null
session_expires_at: timestamp
revoked_at: timestamp|null
```

High-risk approval requires `auth_strength=step_up_mfa` and unexpired step-up.

## Capability

```yaml
contract_version: 1
capability_id: string

control_surface: api|mcp|cli|dom|browser_agent|computer_use|human

auth_scope: object
credential_ref: opaque_ref|null
provider_idempotency: supported|unsupported|unknown
postcondition_observable: boolean
preconditions: object

fallback_routes: [string]
approval_policy: string
network_scope: object
timeout_retry_policy: object
receipt_schema: string
status: active|degraded|disabled
```

If `provider_idempotency != supported` AND `postcondition_observable=false`:
- autonomous retry/replay is forbidden after an ambiguous outcome;
- the action is at least `APPROVAL_REQUIRED`;
- ambiguous completion escalates to human/blocked.

## Deterministic idempotency key

For a material external effect:

`idempotency_key = SHA256(tenant_id || workflow_id || step_id || capability_id || request_hash)`

The same logical workflow step/request MUST produce the same key after process restart or PITR recovery.

## CanonicalEvent

```yaml
contract_version: 1
event_id: uuid
tenant_id: uuid
event_type: string
source_system: string
source_connection_id: uuid|null
source_event_id: string|null

verification_evidence_ref: string|null
```

Rule:
- `FAILED`/`UNKNOWN` authenticity on an event type requiring provider authentication cannot materialize canonical business state.
- It may create a security/source-health event.

## CurrentStateRecord

Required fields:

```yaml

last_event_id: uuid|null
evidence_refs: [string]
```

## AttentionItem

Includes:
- tenant/scope,
- condition key,
- state hash,
- severity,
- owner action required,
- first opened,
- last material change,
- ack/snooze/resolved state,
- non-silenceable flag,
- source/evidence refs.

## DelegationContract

Binds:
- root/parent/task/trace IDs,
- tenant,
- specialist template/version/Git SHA,
- objective,
- trusted/untrusted context refs,
- authority grant,
- allowed/denied tools,
- resource ownership/leases,
- budgets/deadline,
- acceptance criteria,
- required artifacts/evidence,
- review requirement,
- cancellation epoch,
- contract/SOT manifest hash.

Natural language cannot alter control fields.

## WorkerResult

Contains:
- task/tenant/specialist identity,
- status claim,
- summary,

     6	//   ONE explicit gate runs before canonical event materialization.
     7	//   FAILED / missing / unknown authenticity must NOT become canonical state.
     8	//   Verified trusted external events may continue to existing materialization.
     9	//
    10	// Owner classification lock:
    11	//   EXTERNAL/UNKNOWN → trusted connector registry lookup → authenticity
    12	//     verification → PASS may continue; missing/failed/unknown = REJECT.
    13	//   TRUSTED INTERNAL → positive classification from trusted infrastructure
    14	//     provenance only → may bypass external connector authentication.
    15	//   NO caller-supplied field may classify an event as trusted/internal.
    16	//   NO NOT_APPLICABLE passthrough for unknown/external events.
    17	//
    18	// NON-SCOPE: provider-specific live integrations; business-write autonomy.
    19	
    20	import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
    21	import {
    22	  AUTH_REQUIRED_EVENT_TYPES,
    23	  canMaterializeCanonicalState,
    24	  fakeProviderAdapter,
    25	  isAuthenticitySatisfied,
    26	} from '../contracts/events.js';
    27	import { loadConnector, ConnectorValidationError } from '../contracts/connector.js';
    28	import { assertBusinessWriteAutonomyDisabled } from './autonomy.js';
    29	import { buildCurrentStateRecord } from './reconciliation.js';
    30	
    31	export const INBOUND_AUTHENTICITY_GATE_VERSION = 1;
    32	
    33	/** Trusted infrastructure provenance source identifier (not caller-supplied). */
    34	export const TRUSTED_INTERNAL_PROVENANCE_SOURCE = 'agencyos.trusted-runtime';
    35	
    36	/** Event types that trusted internal provenance may authorize (registry only). */
    37	export const TRUSTED_INTERNAL_EVENT_TYPES = Object.freeze([
    38	  'internal.tick',
    39	]);
    40	
    41	/** Deterministic test signature value accepted by the fake verification boundary. */
    42	export const TRUSTED_FAKE_SIGNATURE_HEADER = 'valid-test-sig';
    43	
    44	export class InboundAuthenticityGateError extends Error {
    45	  constructor(code, message, details = null) {
    46	    super(message);
    47	    this.name = 'InboundAuthenticityGateError';
    48	    this.code = code;
    49	    this.details = details;
    50	  }
    51	}
    52	
    53	const UNTRUSTED_VERIFICATION_CLAIM_KEYS = Object.freeze([
    54	  'signaturePresent',
    55	  'signatureValid',
    56	  'authenticity_status',
    57	  'verification_result',
    58	]);
    59	
    60	const UNTRUSTED_CLASSIFICATION_KEYS = Object.freeze([
    61	  'trusted_internal',
    62	  'is_internal',
    63	  'is_trusted',
    64	  'internal',
    65	  'provenance',
    66	  'classification',
    67	  'trusted_provenance',
    68	  'internal_provenance',
    69	  'source_classification',
    70	  'event_classification',
    71	  'trusted',
    72	]);
    73	
    74	function hasUntrustedVerificationClaims(value) {
    75	  if (!value || typeof value !== 'object' || Array.isArray(value)) return false;
    76	  return UNTRUSTED_VERIFICATION_CLAIM_KEYS.some((k) =>
    77	    Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(value, k)
    78	  );
    79	}
    80	
    81	function hasUntrustedClassificationClaims(inbound) {
    82	  if (!inbound || typeof inbound !== 'object') return false;
    83	  if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(inbound, 'connector')
    84	    && inbound.connector !== null
    85	    && typeof inbound.connector === 'object') {
    86	    return true;
    87	  }
    88	  return UNTRUSTED_CLASSIFICATION_KEYS.some((k) =>
    89	    Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(inbound, k)
    90	  );
    91	}
    92	
    93	/**
    94	 * Trusted-internal classification is NOT a caller-mintable object.
    95	 * The sealed entrypoint `processTrustedInternalEvent` is the infrastructure
    96	 * provenance boundary. Plain objects, payload fields, and options such as
    97	 * trusted_provenance cannot authorize an authenticity bypass.
    98	 */
    99	
   100	/**
   101	 * Derive deterministic fake-adapter inputs from raw envelope material only.
   102	 * Never accepts pre-computed verification status from callers.
   103	 */
   104	function deriveFakeAdapterInputsFromRawEnvelope(verification_input = {}) {
   105	  const header = verification_input.signature_header;
   106	  const signaturePresent = header != null && String(header).length > 0;
   107	  const signatureValid = signaturePresent && String(header) === TRUSTED_FAKE_SIGNATURE_HEADER;
   108	  return { signaturePresent, signatureValid };
   109	}
   110	
   111	/**
   112	 * Trusted verification boundary — the ONLY producer of inbound authenticity status.
   113	 * Accepts registry-backed connector metadata plus raw verification_input envelope
   114	 * material. Rejects caller-asserted verification claims (fail closed).
   115	 */
   116	export function verifyInboundSignature({ connector, verification_input = {} } = {}) {
   117	  if (hasUntrustedVerificationClaims(verification_input)) {
   118	    return {
   119	      authenticity_status: 'UNKNOWN',
   120	      authenticity_method: null,
   121	      verification_result: 'UNKNOWN',
   122	      rejection_reason: 'untrusted verification claims rejected; authenticity requires trusted boundary',
   123	    };
   124	  }
   125	
   126	  const ref = connector?.authenticity_verification_ref ?? null;
   127	  if (!ref) {
   128	    return {
   129	      authenticity_status: 'UNKNOWN',
   130	      authenticity_method: null,
   131	      verification_result: 'UNKNOWN',
   132	      rejection_reason: 'missing authenticity_verification_ref on connector',
   133	    };
   134	  }
   135	
   136	  // Phase-1 deterministic fake adapter boundary (acceptance #15).
   137	  if (ref.startsWith('authver://fake/') || ref === 'authver://hmac/fake-adapter') {
   138	    const derived = deriveFakeAdapterInputsFromRawEnvelope(verification_input);
   139	    const result = fakeProviderAdapter(derived);
   140	    return {
   141	      ...result,
   142	      verification_result: result.authenticity_status,
   143	      rejection_reason: isAuthenticitySatisfied(result.authenticity_status)
   144	        ? null
   145	        : `signature verification ${result.authenticity_status.toLowerCase()}`,
   146	    };
   147	  }
   148	
   149	  // Real HMAC refs are not implemented in Phase-1 — fail closed (no fake-HMAC passthrough).
   150	  if (ref.startsWith('authver://hmac/')) {
   151	    return {
   152	      authenticity_status: 'UNKNOWN',
   153	      authenticity_method: ref,
   154	      verification_result: 'UNKNOWN',
   155	      rejection_reason: 'HMAC authenticity verification not available in Phase-1 boundary',
   156	    };
   157	  }
   158	
   159	  return {
   160	    authenticity_status: 'UNKNOWN',
   161	    authenticity_method: ref,
   162	    verification_result: 'UNKNOWN',
   163	    rejection_reason: `unknown authenticity verification ref: ${ref}`,
   164	  };
   165	}
   166	
   167	function buildGateEvidence({
   168	  source_system,
   169	  provider,
   170	  connector_id = null,
   171	  verification_result,
   172	  rejection_reason = null,
   173	  authenticity_method = null,
   174	  classification = null,
   175	  provenance_source = null,
   176	  provenance_boundary = null,
   177	}) {
   178	  return Object.freeze({
   179	    gate_version: INBOUND_AUTHENTICITY_GATE_VERSION,
   180	    source_system,
   181	    provider: provider ?? source_system,
   182	    connector_id,
   183	    verification_result,
   184	    authenticity_method,
   185	    rejection_reason,
   186	    classification,
   187	    provenance_source,
   188	    provenance_boundary,
   189	  });
   190	}
   191	
   192	function rejectGate({
   193	  source_system,
   194	  provider,
   195	  connector_id = null,
   196	  content_trust,
   197	  authenticity_status,
   198	  verification_result,
   199	  rejection_reason,
   200	  authenticity_method = null,
   201	  classification = null,
   202	}) {
   203	  const evidence = buildGateEvidence({
   204	    source_system,
   205	    provider,
   206	    connector_id,
   207	    verification_result,
   208	    rejection_reason,
   209	    authenticity_method,
   210	    classification,
   211	  });
   212	  return Object.freeze({
   213	    accepted: false,
   214	    may_materialize: false,
   215	    authenticity_status,
   216	    content_trust,
   217	    rejection_reason,
   218	    evidence,
   219	  });
   220	}
   221	
   222	/**
   223	 * Load connector exclusively from the trusted tenant registry (never caller objects).
   224	 */
   225	async function loadTrustedRegistryConnector(backend, connector_id) {
   226	  if (typeof connector_id !== 'string' || connector_id.length === 0) {
   227	    return {
   228	      connector: null,
   229	      rejection_reason: 'unknown or unregistered inbound source',
   230	    };
   231	  }
   232	  try {
   233	    const connector = await loadConnector(backend, connector_id);
   234	    return { connector, rejection_reason: null };
   235	  } catch (e) {
   236	    if (e instanceof ConnectorValidationError && /unknown connector/.test(e.message)) {
   237	      return {
   238	        connector: null,
   239	        rejection_reason: `unknown source connector: ${connector_id}`,
   240	      };
   241	    }
   242	    throw e;
   243	  }
   244	}
   245	
   246	/**
   247	 * The explicit inbound authenticity gate for untrusted/external inbound paths.
   248	 * MUST run before materialization. Never accepts caller-supplied trusted/internal
   249	 * classification or verification claims. Trusted-internal events must use the
   250	 * sealed `processTrustedInternalEvent` entrypoint instead.
   251	 *
   252	 * Returns { accepted, may_materialize, evidence, authenticity_status, content_trust, rejection_reason }.
   253	 */
   254	export async function evaluateInboundAuthenticityGate(backend, inbound) {
   255	  assertBusinessWriteAutonomyDisabled();
   256	
   257	  const event_type = inbound.event_type;
   258	  const source_system = inbound.source_system;
   259	  const content_trust = inbound.content_trust ?? 'UNTRUSTED_PAYLOAD';
   260

   346	  test('I. only sealed trusted-internal infrastructure entrypoint may bypass external auth', async () => {
   347	    const inbound = {
   348	      event_type: 'internal.tick',
   349	      source_system: 'agencyos',
   350	      dedupe_key: 'dedupe-internal-trusted-' + randomUUID(),
   351	      content_trust: 'TRUSTED_STRUCTURED',
   352	      materialization: {
   353	        state_key: 'internal:tick',
   354	        domain: 'system',
   355	        subject_ref: 'system',
   356	        value: { tick: 1 },
   357	        state_version: '1',
   358	        max_age_seconds: 60,
   359	      },
   360	    };
   361	
   362	    // Untrusted inbound path → rejected (no forgeable provenance option).
   363	    const rejected = await asRuntimeTenant(db, 'app_runtime', A, async (tx) =>
   364	      processInboundEvent(tx, inbound));
   365	    assert.equal(rejected.gate.accepted, false);
   366	    assert.equal(rejected.materialized, false);
   367	    assert.match(rejected.gate.rejection_reason, /sealed infrastructure entrypoint|connector authenticity/i);
   368	
   369	    // Sealed infrastructure entrypoint → NOT_APPLICABLE bypass.
   370	    const accepted = await asRuntimeTenant(db, 'app_runtime', A, async (tx) =>
   371	      processTrustedInternalEvent(tx, {
   372	        ...inbound,
   373	        dedupe_key: 'dedupe-internal-accepted-' + randomUUID(),
   374	      }));
   375	    assert.equal(accepted.gate.accepted, true);
   376	    assert.equal(accepted.gate.authenticity_status, 'NOT_APPLICABLE');
   377	    assert.equal(accepted.gate.evidence.classification, 'TRUSTED_INTERNAL');
   378	    assert.equal(accepted.gate.evidence.provenance_boundary, 'processTrustedInternalEvent');
   379	    assert.equal(accepted.materialized, true);
   380	
   381	    // Non-allowlisted event_type on sealed entrypoint → rejected.
   382	    const mismatch = await asRuntimeTenant(db, 'app_runtime', A, async (tx) =>
   383	      processTrustedInternalEvent(tx, {
   384	        event_type: 'internal.other',
   385	        source_system: 'agencyos',
   386	        dedupe_key: 'dedupe-internal-other-' + randomUUID(),
   387	      }));
   388	    assert.equal(mismatch.gate.accepted, false);
   389	    assert.match(mismatch.gate.rejection_reason, /not in trusted internal registry/i);
   390	  });

   390	async function requireTenant(backend) {
   391	  const r = await backend.query('SELECT cur_tenant() AS t;');
   392	  const t = r.rows[0]?.t;
   393	  if (!t) {
   394	    throw new InboundAuthenticityGateError('MISSING_TENANT_CONTEXT', 'missing tenant context (fail-closed)');
   395	  }
   396	  return t;
   397	}
   398	
   399	async function findExistingInboundEvent(backend, dedupeKey) {
   400	  const r = await backend.query(
   401	    `SELECT event_id, materialized_state, authenticity_status, content_trust,
   402	            typed_properties->'_inbound_authenticity_gate' AS gate_evidence
   403	     FROM canonical_events
   404	     WHERE dedupe_key = $1;`,
   405	    [dedupeKey]
   406	  );
   407	  return r.rows[0] ?? null;
   408	}
   409	
   410	function gateFromExistingRow(row) {
   411	  const evidence = row.gate_evidence ?? {};
   412	  const may_materialize = row.materialized_state === true;
   413	  return Object.freeze({
   414	    accepted: may_materialize,
   415	    may_materialize,

   417	    content_trust: row.content_trust,
   418	    rejection_reason: evidence.rejection_reason ?? null,
   419	    evidence,
   420	  });

   423	async function persistInboundResult(backend, inbound, gate, { now }) {
   424	  const eventId = inbound.event_id ?? randomUUID();
   425	  const dedupeKey = inbound.dedupe_key ?? `inbound-${eventId}`;
   426	
   427	  const existing = await findExistingInboundEvent(backend, dedupeKey);
   428	  if (existing) {
   429	    return Object.freeze({
   430	      event_id: existing.event_id,
   431	      gate: gateFromExistingRow(existing),
   432	      materialized: existing.materialized_state === true,
   433	      state_record: null,
   434	      replay: true,
   435	    });
   436	  }
   437	
   438	  const typedProperties = {
   439	    ...(inbound.typed_properties ?? {}),
   440	    _inbound_authenticity_gate: gate.evidence,
   441	  };
   442	
   443	  await backend.query(
   444	    `INSERT INTO canonical_events (
   445	       event_id, tenant_id, event_type, source_system, source_connection_id,
   446	       source_event_id, occurred_at, received_at, subject_refs, typed_properties,
   447	       dedupe_key, authenticity_status, authenticity_method, content_trust,
   448	       materialized_state
   449	     ) VALUES (
   450	       $1, cur_tenant(), $2, $3, $4,
   451	       $5, $6::timestamptz, $7::timestamptz, $8::jsonb, $9::jsonb,
   452	       $10, $11, $12, $13, $14
   453	     );`,
   454	    [
   455	      eventId,
   456	      inbound.event_type,
   457	      inbound.source_system,
   458	      inbound.source_connection_id ?? null,
   459	      inbound.source_event_id ?? null,
   460	      inbound.occurred_at ?? null,
   461	      inbound.received_at ?? now,
   462	      JSON.stringify(inbound.subject_refs ?? []),
   463	      JSON.stringify(typedProperties),
   464	      dedupeKey,
   465	      gate.authenticity_status,
   466	      gate.evidence.authenticity_method,
   467	      gate.content_trust,
   468	      gate.may_materialize,
   469	    ]
   470	  );
   471	
   472	  let stateRecord = null;
   473	  if (gate.may_materialize && inbound.materialization) {
   474	    const m = inbound.materialization;
   475	    stateRecord = buildCurrentStateRecord({
   476	      tenant_id: (await requireTenant(backend)),
   477	      state_key: m.state_key,
   478	      domain: m.domain,
   479	      subject_ref: m.subject_ref,
   480	      value: m.value ?? {},
   481	      state_version: m.state_version ?? '1',
   482	      source_system: inbound.source_system,
   483	      as_of: m.as_of ?? now,
   484	      observed_at: m.observed_at ?? now,
   485	      max_age_seconds: m.max_age_seconds ?? 3600,
   486	      last_event_id: eventId,
   487	      evidence_refs: m.evidence_refs ?? [],
   488	    });
   489	
   490	    await backend.query(
   491	      `INSERT INTO current_state_records (
   492	         state_id, tenant_id, state_key, domain, subject_ref, value, state_version,
   493	         source_system, as_of, observed_at, verified_at, max_age_seconds,
   494	         freshness, conflict_status, last_event_id, evidence_refs
   495	       ) VALUES (
   496	         $1, cur_tenant(), $2, $3, $4, $5::jsonb, $6,
   497	         $7, $8::timestamptz, $9::timestamptz, $10::timestamptz, $11,
   498	         $12, $13, $14, $15::jsonb
   499	       )
   500	       ON CONFLICT (tenant_id, state_key) DO UPDATE SET
   501	         value = EXCLUDED.value,
   502	         state_version = EXCLUDED.state_version,
   503	         source_system = EXCLUDED.source_system,
   504	         as_of = EXCLUDED.as_of,
   505	         observed_at = EXCLUDED.observed_at,
   506	         verified_at = EXCLUDED.verified_at,
   507	         max_age_seconds = EXCLUDED.max_age_seconds,
   508	         freshness = EXCLUDED.freshness,
   509	         conflict_status = EXCLUDED.conflict_status,
   510	         last_event_id = EXCLUDED.last_event_id,
   511	         evidence_refs = EXCLUDED.evidence_refs;`,
   512	      [
   513	        randomUUID(),
   514	        stateRecord.state_key,
   515	        stateRecord.domain,
   516	        stateRecord.subject_ref,
   517	        JSON.stringify(stateRecord.value),
   518	        stateRecord.state_version,
   519	        stateRecord.source_system,
   520	        stateRecord.as_of,
   521	        stateRecord.observed_at,
   522	        stateRecord.verified_at,
   523	        stateRecord.max_age_seconds,
   524	        stateRecord.freshness,
   525	        stateRecord.conflict_status,
   526	        stateRecord.last_event_id,
   527	        JSON.stringify(stateRecord.evidence_refs),
   528	      ]
   529	    );
   530	  }
   531	
   532	  return Object.freeze({
   533	    event_id: eventId,
   534	    gate,
   535	    materialized: gate.may_materialize,
   536	    state_record: stateRecord,
   537	    replay: false,
   538	  });
   539	}
   540	
   541	/**
   542	 * Persist canonical event + optional current_state materialization for untrusted
   543	 * inbound events. Gate evaluation ALWAYS precedes INSERT; rejected events never
   544	 * materialize state. Deterministic replay: duplicate dedupe_key returns existing
   545	 * event without a second state transition.
   546	 *
   547	 * Does NOT accept trusted_provenance or any caller-supplied internal classification.
   548	 */
   549	export async function processInboundEvent(
   550	  backend,
   551	  inbound,
   552	  { now = new Date().toISOString() } = {}
   553	) {
   554	  assertBusinessWriteAutonomyDisabled();
   555	  await requireTenant(backend);
   556	  const gate = await evaluateInboundAuthenticityGate(backend, inbound);
   557	  return persistInboundResult(backend, inbound, gate, { now });
   558	}
   559	
   560	/**
   561	 * Sealed trusted-infrastructure entrypoint for positively classified internal events.
   562	 * Calling this API is the provenance boundary — not a forgeable options object.
   563	 * Untrusted callers must use processInboundEvent (fail-closed external path).
   564	 */
   565	export async function processTrustedInternalEvent(
   566	  backend,
   567	  inbound,
   568	  { now = new Date().toISOString() } = {}
   569	) {
   570	  assertBusinessWriteAutonomyDisabled();
   571	  await requireTenant(backend);
   572	
   573	  const event_type = inbound?.event_type;
   574	  const source_system = inbound?.source_system;
   575	  const content_trust = inbound?.content_trust ?? 'TRUSTED_STRUCTURED';
   576	
   577	  if (!event_type || !source_system) {
   578	    throw new InboundAuthenticityGateError(
   579	      'INVALID_INBOUND',
   580	      'event_type and source_system are required'
   581	    );
   582	  }
   583	
   584	  if (hasUntrustedClassificationClaims(inbound) || hasUntrustedVerificationClaims(inbound?.verification)) {
   585	    const gate = rejectGate({
   586	      source_system,
   587	      provider: source_system,
   588	      connector_id: inbound.connector_id ?? null,
   589	      content_trust,
   590	      authenticity_status: 'UNKNOWN',
   591	      verification_result: 'UNKNOWN',
   592	      rejection_reason: 'caller-supplied trusted/internal classification rejected',
   593	    });
   594	    return persistInboundResult(backend, inbound, gate, { now });
   595	  }
   596	
   597	  if (!TRUSTED_INTERNAL_EVENT_TYPES.includes(event_type)) {
   598	    const gate = rejectGate({
   599	      source_system,
   600	      provider: source_system,
   601	      connector_id: inbound.connector_id ?? null,
   602	      content_trust,
   603	      authenticity_status: 'UNKNOWN',
   604	      verification_result: 'UNKNOWN',
   605	      rejection_reason: `event_type not in trusted internal registry: ${event_type}`,
   606	      classification: 'EXTERNAL_OR_UNKNOWN',
   607	    });
   608	    return persistInboundResult(backend, inbound, gate, { now });
   609	  }
   610	
   611	  if (AUTH_REQUIRED_EVENT_TYPES.has(event_type)) {
   612	    const gate = rejectGate({
   613	      source_system,
   614	      provider: source_system,
   615	      connector_id: inbound.connector_id ?? null,
   616	      content_trust,
   617	      authenticity_status: 'UNKNOWN',
   618	      verification_result: 'UNKNOWN',
   619	      rejection_reason: 'auth-required event_type cannot use trusted-internal entrypoint',
   620	      classification: 'EXTERNAL_OR_UNKNOWN',
   621	    });
   622	    return persistInboundResult(backend, inbound, gate, { now });
   623	  }
   624	
   625	  const gate = Object.freeze({
   626	    accepted: true,
   627	    may_materialize: true,
   628	    authenticity_status: 'NOT_APPLICABLE',
   629	    content_trust,
   630	    rejection_reason: null,
   631	    evidence: buildGateEvidence({
   632	      source_system,
   633	      provider: source_system,
   634	      verification_result: 'NOT_APPLICABLE',
   635	      rejection_reason: null,
   636	      authenticity_method: null,
   637	      classification: 'TRUSTED_INTERNAL',
   638	      provenance_source: TRUSTED_INTERNAL_PROVENANCE_SOURCE,
   639	      provenance_boundary: 'processTrustedInternalEvent',
   640	    }),
   641	  });
   642	
   643	  return persistInboundResult(backend, inbound, gate, { now });
   644	}

Owner approved **Stage-1 freeze** and **final merge-readiness review**.

Exact merge candidate prepared at:

**`6fc6d373b4ad142dab0e6d863a38e934c6316db2`**

Merge to `main` is **not** authorized until independent Codex merge-readiness review returns **PASS**.

## Repair after first Codex FAIL

Prior candidate `54b038ebcabc6ef6f40a1bcab5838abef4119213` failed merge-readiness because trusted-internal provenance was forgeable via exported `buildTrustedInternalProvenance` / caller `trusted_provenance` options.

Repair on `6fc6d373b4ad142dab0e6d863a38e934c6316db2`:
- removed caller-mintable provenance builder;
- `processInboundEvent` / `evaluateInboundAuthenticityGate` no longer accept `trusted_provenance`;
- trusted-internal bypass only via sealed `processTrustedInternalEvent`.

// src/runtime/autonomy.js
// Business-write autonomy remains DISABLED until write-path acceptance gates
// pass (docs/master-sot/12_ACCEPTANCE_AND_IMPLEMENTATION.md).
//
// F-08 introduces a trusted executor against a local_fake effect boundary only.
// F-09 introduces DBOS durable workflows (src/runtime/dbos.js) — still with
// live external side effects and business-write autonomy DISABLED.
// F-11 introduces connector registry + opaque credential broker + read-only
// adapters. Writer connectors remain DISABLED (stop condition).
// F-12 introduces observability (receipts/trace, attention, materiality).
// F-13 introduces backup/restore rehearsal (restore must be actually tested).
// F-14 introduces security/privacy acceptance (deletion / PII erasure / tombstone).
// PHASE2_FORBIDDEN_EXECUTION_SURFACES is preserved for Phase 2 regression:
// trusted_executor_material_commit still means live material provider commits.
// 'dbos' / 'connector_registry_persistence' remain on that Phase-2 inventory
// (forbidden then); later phases authorize those surfaces without enabling
// business writes or writer connectors.

export const BUSINESS_WRITE_AUTONOMY = false;

/** F-08/F-09: live external provider commits remain forbidden. */
export const LIVE_EXTERNAL_SIDE_EFFECTS = false;

/** F-09: Postgres-backed durable workflows are in scope; autonomy is not. */
export const DBOS_DURABLE_WORKFLOWS = true;

/** F-11: connector registry persistence is in scope. */
export const CONNECTOR_REGISTRY = true;

/** F-11: opaque credential broker refs are in scope; raw secrets are not. */
export const CREDENTIAL_BROKER_OPAQUE_REFS = true;

/** F-11 stop condition: writer connectors must remain DISABLED. */
export const WRITER_CONNECTORS_ENABLED = false;

/** F-12: observability (materiality, attention, receipt/trace linkage) is in scope. */
export const OBSERVABILITY = true;

/** F-13: backup/restore rehearsal is in scope; unrehearsed restore is a stop. */
export const BACKUP_RESTORE_REHEARSAL = true;

/** F-14: security/privacy acceptance (deletion / PII erasure) is in scope. */
export const SECURITY_PRIVACY_ACCEPTANCE = true;

export function assertBusinessWriteAutonomyDisabled() {
  if (BUSINESS_WRITE_AUTONOMY !== false) {
    throw new Error('BUSINESS_WRITE_AUTONOMY must remain DISABLED');
  }
  if (LIVE_EXTERNAL_SIDE_EFFECTS !== false) {
    throw new Error('LIVE_EXTERNAL_SIDE_EFFECTS must remain DISABLED');
  }
  return true;
}

export function assertWriterConnectorsDisabled() {
  if (WRITER_CONNECTORS_ENABLED !== false) {
    throw new Error('WRITER_CONNECTORS_ENABLED must remain DISABLED (F-11 stop)');
  }
  return true;
}

// Explicit inventory of execution surfaces Phase 2 must NOT introduce.
export const PHASE2_FORBIDDEN_EXECUTION_SURFACES = Object.freeze([
  'live_provider_connection',
  'connector_registry_persistence',
  'credential_broker',
  'trusted_executor_material_commit',
  'dbos',
  'agent_0',
  'external_api_call',
  'external_mcp_call',

]);

---FILE---
        receiptId,
        outcome,
        outcome === 'SUCCEEDED' ? null : `postcondition_${String(post.status).toLowerCase()}`,
      ]
    );
  });

  return {
    status: outcome,
    verification_status: verificationStatus,
    postcondition_status: post.status,
    receipt_id: receiptId,
    effect_id: ledger.effect_id,
    idempotency_key: ledger.idempotency_key,
    commit_token: ledger.commit_token,
    resumed: true,
    claimed_success: outcome === 'SUCCEEDED',
  };
}

  assertBusinessWriteAutonomyDisabled();

    if (err instanceof WritersFrozenError) {
      throw new TrustedExecutorError('WRITERS_FROZEN', err.message);
    }
    throw err;
  }

  const adapter = args?.adapter;
  if (!adapter || adapter.surface !== LOCAL_FAKE_SURFACE) {
    throw new TrustedExecutorError(
      'LIVE_EXTERNAL_FORBIDDEN',
      'only local_fake adapters are permitted in F-08 (no live external side effects)'
    );
  }

  const tenantId = await requireTenant(backend);
  const proposal = await loadProposal(backend, args.proposal_id);
  if (!proposal) {
    throw new TrustedExecutorError('PROPOSAL_NOT_FOUND', `proposal ${args.proposal_id} not found`);
  }
  if (proposal.tenant_id !== tenantId) {
    throw new TrustedExecutorError('TENANT_MISMATCH', 'proposal tenant does not match trusted context');
  }
